There will be 9 rounds. Each round, you’ll submit one song that fits the theme, then we’ll all divvy up points—and one downvote—among the submissions.

Themes can be broad or specific. Examples: a song you loved as a teenager and still listen to, a song that pumps you up, a great song nobody else has heard, a song released the year you were born, a breakup song, an instrumental track, or a song that reminds you of a specific time in your life.

FYI - I am giving Pallets until midnight to join up - then I will submit my song - which will trigger the end of the submission phase for  round 1 - so a playlist will be waiting when you awake tomorrow, along with an ability to vote.  After heated debate - we decided on the following vote counts and guidelines:
- Voting consists of up votes and down votes
- You must cast all your votes in order to submit
- You have 7 up votes  and 2 down votes to distribute
- Maximum of 4 up votes on any individual song
- No maximum on down votes per song (ie you can put both down votes on 1 song or do 1 down vote each on 2 separate songs)
- Make sure you submit the slate of votes (press the submit button at the bottom) after you distribute all 7 up votes and 2 down votes
- You are encouraged to provide comments for any songs you feel strongly about (especially down votes)
- Once the voting phase commences - you will have 4 days to get your votes in
